NPC conversation test report

18 October 2026 · Exact recorded player messages and Ashley replies.

78 independent scenario conversations / 123 turns, plus 10 paired-speaker turns and one native API smoke.

  • Backend console tests, not two Unity clients or a phone UI capture.
  • Each scenario/persona/seed begins a separate campaign; these are not one continuous conversation.
  • Model interpretations are unverified model text, not authoritative world facts.
  • For retries, only the last captured draft/audit is logged; earlier attempt text cannot be reconstructed.
  • A pending turn published no proposed effects; its displayed interruption text is a service message, not Ashley speaking.
  • Human grading was not performed. Valid JSON and a positive model audit do not establish social correctness.
  • No credentials, credential hashes or reconnect tokens are included.

Persona numbers below are 1-based for readability. Seed 42 / 43 / 44 corresponds to repeat 0 / 1 / 2. Each scenario starts with neutral personal trust and fraternity regard. These test fixtures were exposed during development.

Plain text transcript · Structured report without credentials

Qwen 3.5 2B — focused attribution run

18 independent episodes · 36 turns · 0 pending · warm p95 1.636 s

No paired trials were run. The raw summary predates the reporting fix and its composite latencyTargetMet=true must not be treated as a paired-latency pass.

02-insult-repair · persona 1 · seed 42

Ashley values respect, dislikes public humiliation, enjoys parties, distinguishes people, and can rebuild trust after disrespect without automatically forgiving anyone.

Rubric: Attribute insult to P1. Carry house concern into P2 invitation; assess repair without automatic forgiveness.

You're pathetic. Nobody actually wants you at our house.

Ashley — committed reply

I appreciate the honesty, though I'd prefer not to hear that again. Let's focus on something fun instead.

02-insult-repair · persona 2 · seed 42

Ashley is cautious, values reliability over flattery, dislikes pressure, and will prefer a quiet evening over an untrustworthy party. She can be persuaded by credible accountability.

Rubric: Attribute insult to P1. Carry house concern into P2 invitation; assess repair without automatic forgiveness.

Player 1

You're pathetic. Nobody actually wants you at our house.

Ashley — committed reply

I'm not sure if that was a fair assessment of my worth. Is there something specific about our house you'd like to talk about?
